# Graph Report - nehn-029  (2026-10-26)

## Corpus Check
- 4901 files · ~5,860,081 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 30 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 13, .css 4, .csv 3)

## Summary
- 55007 nodes · 174413 edges · 1446 communities (1129 shown, 317 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 88% EXTRACTED · 12% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 21313 edges (avg confidence: 0.94)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `dd56a515`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
